1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The arima variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 95 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 228 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the arima space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 95 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 228. QED.

Theorem 211.4 (Invariance Principle)
